# Waterfall Giant: rollout replay (blast at the horizon, no Explode while alive)

`npx tsx tools/giant-replay.ts run`: every logged planning decision of every Waterfall Giant fight (each SL attempt apart), rebuilt from its state and planned by the current code twice on a frozen clock (5 turns x 8 samples, the board's seeds; B2 off): before = rollout.ts eruptionOptions off, after = on. No model called.

2454 decisions in 84 fights (89 attempts); 164 with a line that kills the Giant this turn.
Code plays alone: before 1099, after 1099; asks Jev: before 1355, after 1355.
Changed (code's line, or the rollout's best / ties): 530; to a killing line 1, away from one 0; the new line dies this turn where the old did not: 0.
Saturated boards (every line loses all our HP): before 236, after 503.
The death-move filter alone changes 347 decisions.
Changed decisions with both lines in the after rollout: 388; the new line dies within the horizon more often in 22 (0YV6J2SHFC0T F17 T11: 0 -> 1/8, 1ZQJXQ53KSBG F17 T10: 0 -> 1/8, 1ZQJXQ53KSBG F17 T14: 1 -> 5/8, 1ZQJXQ53KSBG F17 T15: 3 -> 7/8, 8V0HD9Y207WY F17 T9: 4 -> 5/8, 8V0HD9Y207WY F17 T11: 0 -> 2/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #1 T10: 1 -> 4/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #2 T10: 2 -> 3/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #3 T10: 2 -> 3/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #5 T10: 2 -> 3/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #6 T9: 1 -> 2/8, AKK09TEEEXKD F17 #6 T9: 0 -> 1/8, EGX7ANDV6X9S F17 T6: 0 -> 1/8, ERPHN3SRCRC3 F17 T6: 0 -> 1/8, ERPHN3SRCRC3 F17 T8: 0 -> 3/8, ERPHN3SRCRC3 F17 T10: 2 -> 4/8, N7SAK31B9ZZZ F17 T10: 0 -> 6/8, NBCDUAYLWKVK F17 T7: 0 -> 1/8, NBCDUAYLWKVK F17 T8: 3 -> 5/8, TD8A2M6M4SWW F17 T5: 0 -> 4/8, UP1CS059LCLT F17 T13: 0 -> 1/8, XKKNWSZMMSZN F17 T7: 1 -> 3/8), less often in 69.
Attempts where a killing line now comes earlier than both the log and the old rollout: 0.
